import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def _next_pow2(m):
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
```

`fft_conv_full(x)`:
```
n=x.shape[0]
K=2*n-1
L=_next_pow2(K)
F=np.fft.rfft(x,L)
c=np.fft.irfft(F*F,L)[:K]
c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
return c
```
For cross-plus:
```
def fft_cross_plus(p,w):
    n=len(p); K=len(w); L=_next_pow2(K)
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
    Q=np.fft.rfft(w,L)
    h=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(P)*Q,L)[:n]
    return np.maximum(h,0.0) # should be nonneg if p,w nonneg; clip small ie
```
Is there no L factor? Let's verify with simple p=[1,0], w=[1,0,0], L=4. P rfft? DFT? ifft(conj(P)Q)=p = [1,0]. h0=1. good. p=[1,1], w=[1,0,0], K=3, L=4. Q as above. h0=p0*1+p1*0=1; h1=p0*0+p1*0=0. ifft? likely. Good.

But wait w length K may be zero at end. `np.fft.rfft(w, L)` zero pads. Good.

For p*LSE objective:
```
def lse_max_from_conv(c, tau):
    cmax = c.max()
    z = (c - cmax)/tau
    # stable logsumexp
    zmax = z.max() # 0
    lse = cmax + tau * (zmax + np.log(np.exp(z - zmax).sum()))
    return lse, w? 
```

Resampling function:
```
def resample_sequence(a, m):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    n=len(a)
    if m==n: return a/a.sum()
    x_old=(np.arange(n)- (n-1)/2)
    # To preserve mass, use values? If uniform, resample density.
    y_old=a/a.sum()
    # Use log for smooth: logv=np.log(y_old + eps)
    y_new=np.exp(np.interp(np.linspace(-(m-1)/2,(m-1)/2,m), x_old, np.log(y_old+1e-300)))
    y_new/=y_new.sum()
    return y_new
```
If parent has zeros, log eps. Could introduce tiny everywhere, maybe okay. Or linearly interpolate probability: `np.interp` of p; but rescaling sum may not conserve exactly; normalize. Log may overweight zeros. Use linear of p + small. Maybe shape. Use both: linear and log. For given parent, generate candidates: same length, linear resample to 4096, 8192. We can test.

def _score_fft(a):
    arr=np.asarray(a,float)
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr),arr,0.0); arr=np.clip(arr,0,0)
    s=arr.sum(); if s<1e-12: return inf,None
    p=arr/s; n=len(p)
    if n <= 700: c=np.convolve(p,p)
    else: c=_fft_conv(p)
    M=float(c.max())
    return float(2.0*n*M), p
```

`_softmax(x)`:
```
def _softmax(x):
    x=np.asarray(x,float)
    x=x-x.max()
    e=np.exp(x)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return uniform
    return e/s
```

L-BFGS functions:
```
def _lbfgs_obj(x):
    p=_softmax(x)
    c=_fft_conv(p)
    M,_ = _lse_value(c, _LBFGS_TAU)
    return M  # or 2nM

def _lbfgs_grad(x):
    p=_softmax(x)
    c=_fft_conv(p)
    _,w=_lse_weights(c, _LBFGS_TAU)
    n=len(p); tau=_LBFGS_TAU
    h=_fft_cross_plus(p,w)
    g=(2.0/tau)*h
    return p*(g - np.dot(p,g))
```
Need `_lse_value` and `_lse_weights` top-level:
```
def _lse_value(c, tau):
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    z=(c-cmax)/tau
    zmax=np.max(z)
    vals=np.exp(z-zmax)
    lse=cmax+tau*(zmax+np.log(vals.sum()))
    return lse, None
```
For weights:
```
def _lse_weights(c,tau):
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    z=(c-cmax)/tau
    zmax=np.max(z)
    w=np.exp(z-zmax)
    w /= w.sum()
    return w
```
If tau very small, z huge negative; exp underflow okay. If tau 0? avoid.

Could combine to avoid recomputing c for obj/grad? no.

`_refine_lbfgs` code top-level:
```
def _refine_lbfgs(p, max_seconds):
    n=len(p); x=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300)); best_p=p.copy(); best_m=fft_max(p); deadline=time.monotonic()+max_seconds; cmax0=max(fft_max(p),1e-12)
    factors=[0.3,0.15,0.08,0.04,0.02,0.01,0.005]
    for f in factors:
        if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
        tau=max(1e-10, f*cmax0)
        _LBFGS_TAU=tau
        # maybe if tau smaller than cmax*1e-5 stop
        try:
           res=minimize(_lbfgs_obj, x, jac=_lbfgs_grad, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':250, 'maxfun':500, 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-11, 'maxls':30})
           x=np.asarray(res.x,float)
        except Exception:
           break
        # ensure x finite
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): break
        p2=_softmax(x)
        # floor tiny
        p2=np.maximum(p2,0.0)
        s=p2.sum(); if s<1e-12: break
        p2/=s
        m2=_fft_max(p2)
        if np.isfinite(m2) and m2 < best_m:
            best_m=m2; best_p=p2.copy()
        # if no improvement maybe continue
    return best_p
```
Use `_fft_max` separate from `_score_fft`.

   c=_fft_conv(p); return float(c.max())
```

def _random_smooth_candidate(n, rng):
   t=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False)
   x=(2*t-1)*rng.uniform(0,2) + rng.uniform(-0.5,0.5)*np.cos(2*np.pi*rng.integers(1,5)*t)
   M=rng.integers(10,40)
   freqs=rng.choice(np.arange(1,max(2, M+1)), size=M, replace=False)
   for f in freqs:
       # odd frequencies for antisymmetry maybe
       if f%2==1: amp=rng.uniform(0.5,3.0)
       else: amp=rng.uniform(-1,1)
       phase=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
       x += amp*np.cos(f*np.pi*t + phase)
   return x
```

Let's design `_polish_subgradient(p, max_seconds)` more concretely:
```
def _polish_subgradient(p, max_seconds):
    global _RNGRESH? use top-level rng? 
    p=np.array(p,float); p=np.maximum(p,1e-300); p/=p.sum()
    n=len(p)
    best_p=p.copy(); best_m=_fft_max(p)
    deadline=time.monotonic()+max_seconds
    no_improve=0; alpha=0.05
    top_k_base = max(16, min(256, n//32))
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       c,_,_ = _fft_conv_info? We can compute c and M.
       M=float(c.max())
       if M < best_m*(1-1e-12): best_m=M; best_p=p.copy(); no_improve=0
       if M - best_m < 1e-14: maybe break? But best_m updated to M.
       # active weights
       K=top_k_base; adjust if no_improve: K=int(K*rng.uniform(0.5,2)) caps.
       thresh = M * (1 - rng.uniform(0.01,0.25))
       idx=np.flatnonzero(c >= thresh)
       if len(idx) < 4: idx=np.argsort(c)[-K:]
       if len(idx)>K: idx=np.argsort(c)[::-1][:K]
       w_active = c[idx] - thresh
       if w_active.sum() <=0: w_active=np.ones(len(idx))
       lam=np.zeros(len(c)); lam[idx]=w_active/np.sum(w_active)
       h=_fft_cross_plus(p,lam)
       g=h - np.mean(h)
       scale = np.max(np.abs(g))
       if not finite or scale<1e-15: break
       base = alpha * (M / scale) # alpha maybe 1?
       trials = [base*0.25, base*0.5, base, base*2, base*4]
       # include fixed small [1e-4,...]
       best_trial=None
       log? use multiplicative:
       for step in trials:
          rel=-step*g
          rel-=rel.max()
          e=np.exp(np.clip(rel,-700,700))
          p2=p*e; s=p2.sum()
          if s<=0: continue
          p2/=s
          m2=_fft_max(p2)
          if m2 < best_trial_score: store
       if best_trial improved by >1e-15:
          p=best_trial; no_improve=0; if alpha<0.5: alpha*=1.2
       else:
          no_improve+=1
          if no_improve>20:
             # random perturb
             p2=_softmax(np.log(padding) + rng.normal(0,0.01, n))
             if _fft_max(p2) < best_m: p=p2; best...
             no_improve=0
          alpha*=0.8
       if time check every few: exact? Not inside.
    return best_p

Let's write helper specifics:

```
def _next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(m)-1).bit_length()
```

`_fft_conv(a)`:
```
n = len(a)
K=2*n-1
L=_next_pow2(K)
fa=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, L)[:K]
# remove tiny imaginary? irfft real
c[np.abs(c)<1e-300]=0? no.
c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
return c
```
Potential memory for a huge n? n=8192 L=16384? Actually K=16383, L=16384. Good. n=10000 L=32768. okay.

`_fft_max(a)`:
```
n=len(a)
if n <= 600:
    c=np.convolve(a,a)
    return max(0.0,float(np.max(c)))
c=_fft_conv(a); return float(np.max(c))
```

`_score_fft(a)`:
```
arr=np.asarray(a,float)
arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
arr=np.clip(arr,0,None)
s=arr.sum()
if s <=1e-12: return inf,None
p=arr/s; n=len(p)
if n <=600: M=float(np.max(np.convolve(p,p)))
else: M=_fft_max(p)
return 2.0*n*M, p
```
For n >600 uses `_fft_max` which already uses FFT. To avoid double, just M=_fft_max(p). Good.

`_try_sequence(a)`:
```
global _BEST_SCORE, _BEST_SEQ

if arr is None or arr.sum()<0.01: return inf

Resample:
```
def _resample(a, m, mode=0):
    a=_clean_array(a)
    n=len(a)
    if m<=0 or n==m: return a
    if np.sum(a)==0: return None
    if mode==0: # linear p
        old_x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
        new_x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m)
        # avoid exact endpoints? for p sequence length m, positions maybe i/(n-1)
        y=np.interp(new_x, old_x, a)
    else:
        p=a/np.sum(a)
        old_x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
        new_x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m)
        logv=np.interp(new_x, old_x, np.log(p+1e-300))
        y=np.exp(logv - logv.max())
    y = np.maximum(y,0)
    s=y.sum()
    if s<=0: return None
    return y/s if mode==1 else y/np.sum(y)
```
For linear, y values not normalized density exactly but normalize. Log-mode preserves relative. We can generate both and try.

Random x:
```
def _random_x(n, rng, stride=1):
    t=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n,endpoint=False)
    x=np.empty(n)
    # base linear asymmetry
    lin = rng.uniform(-2.0,2.0)
    x = lin*(2.0*t-1.0)
    # maybe quadratic
    x += rng.uniform(-1,1)*((t-0.5)**2*4 -0.5)
    # Fourier
    M = int(rng.integers(8, 48))
    # choose frequencies; ensure some odd
    for _ in range(M):
       f = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, min(200, max(2, n//8)))))
       # bias odd
       if rng.random()<0.8 and f%2==0: f += 1
       amp = rng.uniform(0.2, 3.0) * (1.5 if f%2==1 else 0.5)
       phase = rng.uniform(0, 2*np.pi)
       x += amp * np.cos(f*np.pi*t + phase)
    # normalize amplitude
    scale = 4.0 / max(1.0, float(np.max(np.abs(x))))
    return x*scale
```
But Fourier with integer f on t endpoint=False; frequency fπ. If f even, symmetric; odd antisymmetric. Good. Could generate full random smooth via FFT: random coefficients in frequency domain low-pass. This maybe better. We can implement:
- n length, random real coefficients with random phases for frequencies up to M, plus maybe anti-symmetric. Simpler: `freq = np.arange(n); mask = np.abs(freq - n/2) < M; x = random real?` To ensure smooth, generate low-frequency random via inverse FFT. But p=exp(x) real. Let's implement robust:

def _randomSmoothX(n,rng,M=40,amp=2):
    x=np.zeros(n)
    f=np.arange(n)
    # choose low freq indices <=M and their negatives
    idx=list(range(1,M+1)) + list(range(n-M,n))
    x=np.zeros(n)

       ph=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
       x += 2*c*np.cos(2*np.pi*k*np.arange(n)/n + ph)?
```

```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_BEST_SCORE = float('inf')
_BEST_SEQ = None
_LBFGS_TAU = 1e-6

def _next_pow2(m):
    m=int(m)
    if m <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
```

`_clean_array`:
```
def _clean_array(a):
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.minimum(arr, 1000.0)
    return arr
```

`_fft_conv`, `_fft_max` as above.

`_fft_cross_plus`:
```
def _fft_cross_plus(p, w):
    n = len(p)
    L = _next_pow2(max(len(w), n))
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    Q = np.fft.rfft(w, L)
    h = np.fft.irfft(np.conj(P) * Q, L)[:n]
    h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
    return h
```

Let's draft `_polish_subgrad`:
```
def _polish_subgrad(p, max_seconds, rng):
    p = _clean_array(p)
    if p is None: return None
    s=p.sum()
    if s<1e-12: return None
    p = p/s
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-300); p/=p.sum()
    n=len(p)
    best_p=p.copy()
    best_m=_fft_max(p)
    deadline=time.monotonic()+max_seconds
    no_improve=0
    top_k = max(24, min(300, n//20))
    iters=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline and iters < 20000:
        iters +=1
        c = _fft_conv(p)
        M = float(np.max(c))
        if M < best_m - 1e-15:
            best_m=M; best_p=p.copy()
            no_improve=0
        # if close to best (which equals M if p best) no improvement? Since p always best, M==best_m. If p updated.
        # active lam
        frac = float(rng.uniform(0.02, 0.30))
        kk = int(top_k * rng.uniform(0.5, 2.0))
        kk = max(8, min(400, kk))
        thresh = max(0.0, M * (1.0 - frac))
        idx = np.flatnonzero(c >= thresh)
        if idx.size < 4:
            idx = np.argsort(c)[-kk:]
        elif idx.size > kk:
            idx = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:kk]
        vals = c[idx] - thresh
        if vals.sum() <= 0:
            vals = np.ones(len(idx))
        lam = np.zeros(len(c))
        lam[idx] = vals / vals.sum()
        h = _fft_cross_plus(p, lam)
        g = h - float(np.mean(h))
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
        if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale < 1e-15:
            break
        base = max(1e-6, 0.2 * M / scale)  # exponent scale
        steps = [base*0.25, base*0.5, base, base*2.0, base*4.0]
        best_step_score = best_m
        best_p2 = None
        for st in steps:
            if st <=0: continue
            rel = -st * g
            rel -= float(np.max(rel))
            e = np.exp(np.clip(rel, -50.0, 50.0))
            p2 = p * e
            ss = float(p2.sum())
            if ss <= 0: continue
            p2 = p2 / ss
            # Maybe floor zeros? p*e zeros remain zero; okay.
            m2 = _fft_max(p2)
            if m2 < best_step_score:
                best_step_score = m2
                best_p2 = p2.copy()
        if best_p2 is not None and best_step_score < best_m - 1e-16:
            p = best_p2
            best_m = best_step_score
            best_p = p.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 30:
                # random perturb
                logp = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
                improved=False
                for noise in [0.005,0.01,0.02,0.002]:
                    logp2 = logp + rng.normal(0.0, noise, n)
                    p2 = _softmax(logp2)
                    p2 = np.maximum(p2,0.0)
                    s2=float(p2.sum())
                    if s2>0:
                       p2/=s2
                    m2=_fft_max(p2)
                    if m2 < best_m:
                       best_m=m2; best_p=p2.copy(); p=p2.copy(); improved=True; break
                if not improved:
                    # mixture with current best? no change
                    pass
                no_improve=0
    return best_p
```
But in loop, `M` should equal best_m since p is best. If p not updated, no improve. Good.

Potential problem: If all p positive, multiplicative never re-enters zero; floor 1e-300 so tiny re-entry possible. Good.
`_fft_max` in trials recomputes conv; we can use `_fft_conv` maybe not need full. okay.

Maybe line search should compare to current `M` not `best_m`; same. If no trial improves due to tiny noise, stagnate. Random perturbation can help but may not find. L-BFGS more effective.

`_project_simplex` top-level:
```
def _project_simplex(v):
    n=len(v); u=np.sort(v)[::-1]; cssv=np.cumsum(u)-1; ind=np.nonzero(u*(np.arange(1,n+1))+1-cssv>0)[0][-1]; theta=(cssv[ind]-1)/(ind+1.0); w=np.maximum(v-theta,0); return w
```

for st in steps:
   p2 = _project_simplex(p - st*g)
   if p2.sum <=0 continue
   m2=...
```

def _add_to_pool(pool, arr, score, cap=40):
    if arr is None: return
    if not np.isfinite(score): return
    pool.append((float(score), arr.copy()))

        del pool[cap:]

def _is_similar(a,b):
   if a is None or b is None or len(a)!=len(b): return False
   na=np.linalg.norm(a); nb=np.linalg.norm(b)
   if na<1e-12 or nb<1e-12: return True
   return np.linalg.norm(a-b)/na < 0.02
```

Let's write `_random_search`:
```
def _random_search(n, max_seconds, pool, rng, max_cap=30):
    deadline=time.monotonic()+max_seconds
    count=0
    max_iter=int(max(100, min(5000, int(max_seconds*40))))
    # Add Dirichlet candidates
    for alpha in [0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0]:
       p=rng.dirichlet(np.ones(n)*alpha); sc,_=_score_fft(p); _add_to_pool(pool,p,sc,max_cap)
       count+=1
    while time.monotonic()<deadline and count<max_iter:
       x=_random_x(n,rng)
       p=_softmax(x)
       sc, _ = _score_fft(p)
       _add_to_pool(pool,p,sc,max_cap)
       count+=1
       if count % 500 == 0: print(...)
```

`_add_common_candidates(n, pool, rng?)`:
```
def _add_common_candidates(n, pool, cap=30):
    t=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False)
    u=np.ones(n)
    def addp(p): sc,_=_score_fft(p); _add_to_pool(pool,p,sc,cap)
    def normal(p): s=p.sum(); return p/s if s>0 else None
    # uniform
    addp(np.ones(n))
    for a in [-3,-2,-1,-0.5,0,0.5,1,2,3]:
       p=np.exp(a*(2*t-1)); addp(normal(p))
    for a in [0.5,1,1.5,2]:
       p=np.exp(a*np.cos(np.pi*t)); addp(normal(p))
       p=np.exp(a*np.cos(np.pi*(t-0.25))); addp(normal(p))
    for aa in [0.5,1,2]:
       for bb in [0.2,0.5,1,2]:
          p=(t+1e-6)**aa * (1.0-t+1e-6)**bb
          addp(normal(p))
    for a in [-2,-1,-0.5,0,0.5,1,2]:
       p=1.0 + a*(2*t-1)
       if np.min(p) < 0: p=np.maximum(p,1e-6)
       addp(normal(p))
       p=1.0 + a*(2*t-1) + (a*0.2)*np.cos(2*np.pi*t)
       addp(normal(p))
```
No lambda. Use helper `_add_p_scored(pool,p,cap)` top-level.

def _add_p_scored(pool,p,cap=30):
   if p is None: return

   _add_to_pool(pool,p,sc,cap)

`_lbfgs_obj`:
```
def _lbfgs_obj(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    p = _softmax(x)
    c = _fft_conv(p)
    lse = _lse_val(c, _LBFGS_TAU)
    return float(lse)
```
`_lse_val` returns float.

`_lse_val`:
```
def _lse_val(c, tau):
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if tau <= 0: return cmax
    z = (c - cmax) / tau
    zmax = float(np.max(z))
    vals = np.exp(z - zmax)
    return cmax + tau * (zmax + float(np.log(vals.sum())))
```
Since zmax=0 usually, okay.

`_lse_weight`:
```
def _lse_weight(c,tau):
    cmax=float(np.max(c)); z=(c-cmax)/tau; zmax=float(np.max(z)); w=np.exp(z-zmax); s=w.sum();
    if s<=0: w=np.zeros(len(c)); w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1
    else: w/=s
    return w
```

`_lbfgs_grad`:
```
def _lbfgs_grad(x):
    x=...
    p=_softmax(x)
    c=_fft_conv(p)
    w=_lse_weight(c,_LBFGS_TAU)
    h=_fft_cross_plus(p,w)
    g_p=(2.0/_LBFGS_TAU)*h
    return p*(g_p - np.dot(p,g_p))
```
Potential division by tau if tau 0; set tau min. Good.

`_refine_lbfgs` uses global tau. If minimize calls with x not finite, returns maybe. We check after.
```
def _refine_lbfgs(p, max_seconds):
    p = _clean_array(p); ... normalize
    n=len(p)
    x = np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300)); x -= np.mean(x); x=np.clip(x,-80,80)
    best_p=p.copy(); best_m=_fft_max(p)
    deadline=time.monotonic()+max_seconds
    cmax0=max(best_m,1e-12)
    factors = [0.5,0.25,0.12,0.06,0.03,0.015,0.008,0.004]
    for f in factors:
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       tau = max(1e-12, f*cmax0)
       _LBFGS_TAU = float(tau)
       # maybe run 1 or 2 minimize passes
       try:
          opts = {'maxiter': 250, 'maxfun': 500, 'ftol':1e-12, 'gtol':1e-10, 'maxls':20}
          if n > 8000: opts = {'maxiter':150,...}
          res = minimize(_lbfgs_obj, x, jac=_lbfgs_grad, method='L-BFGS-B', options=opts)
          x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
       except Exception:
          break
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): break
       x = np.clip(x, -80, 80)
       x -= float(np.mean(x))
       p2 = _softmax(x)
       p2 = np.maximum(p2, 0.0)
       s = float(p2.sum())
       if s <= 0: break
       p2 /= s
       m2 = _fft_max(p2)
       if np.isfinite(m2) and m2 < best_m:
          best_m = m2; best_p = p2.copy()
       # optional: if improvement < tiny and tau small, break
    # final exact-ish? no
    return best_p
```

```
cmax_ref = max(best_m,1e-12)

   tau=max(1e-12, factor*cmax_ref)
```

import time

_BEST_SCORE=...

def propose_candidate(budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    global _BEST_SCORE, _BEST_SEQ
    start=...
    deadline=...
    rng=...
    # baseline
    _try_sequence(np.ones(1024))
    # parent
    parent=None
    try:
       hs = globals().get('height_sequence_1')
       if hs is not None:
          parent = _clean_array(hs)
          if parent is not None:
             _try_sequence(parent)
             for m in [2048,4096,8192]:
                 if time.monotonic() < deadline-60:
                    for mode in (0,1):
                       r=_resample(parent,m,mode)
                       if r is not None:
                          _try_sequence(r)
    except Exception as exc: print...
    pool=[]
    if _BEST_SEQ is not None: _add_p_scored(pool,_BEST_SEQ,40)
    # common candidates n=4096
    if time.monotonic() < deadline-300:
       _add_common_candidates(4096,pool)
    # random search budgets
    now=time.monotonic()
    tb1 = min(120.0, max(20.0, (deadline-now)*0.08))
    if time.monotonic() < deadline-300:
       _random_search(4096, tb1, pool, rng)
    tb2 = min(60.0, max(10.0, (deadline-now)*0.04))
    if time.monotonic() < deadline-240:
       _random_search(2048, tb2, pool, rng)
    # exact top pool
    if pool:
       pool.sort(key=_score_key)
       n_exact_top = min(20, len(pool))
       for i in range(n_exact_top):
           if time.monotonic() < deadline-300:
               _try_sequence(pool[i][1])
    # refine starts
    now=time.monotonic()
    reserve = max(200.0, (deadline-now)*0.18)
    refine_budget = max(0.0, deadline - now - reserve - 30.0)
    max_starts = int(min(5, max(1, refine_budget / 180.0 + 1)))
    starts=[]
    if _BEST_SEQ is not None:
       st=_prep_opt_start(_BEST_SEQ)
       if st is not None: starts.append(st)
    if pool:
       for sc,arr in pool:
           if len(starts)>=max_starts: break
           st=_prep_opt_start(arr)
           if st is None: continue
           if any(_is_similar(st, o) for o in starts): continue
           starts.append(st)
    # If no starts, use random x
    if not starts:
       starts.append(_softmax(_random_x(4096, rng)))
    if len(starts)>0 and refine_budget>30:
       per = max(30.0, refine_budget/len(starts))
       for idx,p in enumerate(starts):
           if time.monotonic() > deadline - reserve - 30: break
           if time.monotonic() + 30 > deadline: break
           print(...)
           q = _refine_lbfgs(p, min(per, deadline-time.monotonic()-reserve-30))
           if q is not None:
              _try_sequence(q)
    # resample best to other lengths
    if _BEST_SEQ is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline-120:
       cur_len=len(_BEST_SEQ)
       for m in [4096,6144,8192]:
           if m != cur_len and time.monotonic() < deadline-120:
              r=_resample(_BEST_SEQ,m,1)
              if r is not None:
                 _try_sequence(r)
    # final extra LBA on best if time
    if _BEST_SEQ is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline-120:
       q=_prep_opt_start(_BEST_SEQ)
       if q is not None:
          q2=_refine_lbfgs(q, min(200.0, max(20.0, deadline-time.monotonic()-60)))
          if q2 is not None: _try_sequence(q2)
    # polish
    if _BEST_SEQ is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline-60:
       t=max(20.0, min(300.0, deadline-time.monotonic()-30))
       q=_polish_subgrad(_prep_opt_start(_BEST_SEQ), t, rng)
       if q is not None: _try_sequence(q)
       # maybe polish first pool
       if pool and time.monotonic() < deadline-60:
          st=_prep_opt_start(pool[0][1])
          if st is not None and not _is_similar(st,_BEST_SEQ):
             q=_polish_subgrad(st, max(20.0, min(120.0, deadline-time.monotonic()-30)), rng)
             if q is not None: _try_sequence(q)
    # final safety
    if _BEST_SEQ is None:
       _BEST_SEQ = np.ones(1024)
       _BEST_SCORE = float('inf')
    # ensure finite
    final = _clean_array(_BEST_SEQ)
    if final is None or final.size ==0:
       final=np.ones(1024)
    s=final.sum()
    if s<=0: final=np.ones(1024); s=1024
    final=final/s
    print(f"Final score (tracked): {_BEST_SCORE}")
    return [float(v) for v in final]

def _prep_opt_start(a):
    arr=_clean_array(a)
    if arr is None: return None
    s=arr.sum()
    if s<1e-12: return None
    p=arr/s
    n=len(p)
    if n < 3000:
       r=_resample(p,4096,0)
       if r is not None: return r
       return p
    elif n > 9000:
       r=_resample(p,8192,0)
       if r is not None: return r
       return p
    return p

`_anchor_obj(x)`:
```
def _anchor_obj(x):
    p=_ANCHOR_P; n=len(p); L=_ANCHOR_L
    q=_softmax(x)
    Fq=np.fft.rfft(q,L)
    c_self=np.fft.irfft(Fq*Fq,L)[:2*n-1]
    c_cross=np.fft.irfft(_ANCHOR_PF*Fq,L)[:2*n-1]
    vals=np.concatenate((c_self, c_cross))
    vals = np.maximum(vals,0.0)
    return _lse_val(vals, _LBFGS_TAU)
```
`_anchor_grad(x)`:
Need w_combined. Repeats FFT. To reduce recompute, could compute weights from vals; then w_self = w[:K], w_cross=w[K:]. But if `_anchor_obj` and grad separate, okay.
```
def _anchor_grad(x):
   q, c_self, c_cross, Fq... same
   vals=concat; w_all=_lse_weight(vals,tau); w_self=w_all[:K]; w_cross=w_all[K:]
   h_self=_fft_cross_plus(q,w_self)
   h_cross=_fft_cross_plus(_ANCHOR_P, w_cross)  # since anchor length n, w length K
   g_q=(2.0/tau)*h_self + (1.0/tau)*h_cross
   return q*(g_q - np.dot(q,g_q))
```
But note if w_all is softmax over combined vector, weights sum1. Then self w terms sum maybe ≤1, cross sum. Derivative of LSE with same tau: w normalized over combined. Correct.

`_refine_anchor(anchor_p, max_seconds)`:

    p=_clean_array(anchor_p); normalize; set anchor
    x=np.log(p); cmax0=_fft_max(p)
    best_p=p.copy(); best_m=_fft_max(p)

       p2=softmax; m2=_fft_max(p2); if m2<best_m update
    return best_p

`_next_pow2`:
```
def _next_pow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
```

`_clean_array` as above.

`_fft_conv`:
```
def _fft_conv(a):
    n = int(len(a))
    if n <= 0: return np.array([])
    K=2*n-1; L=_next_pow2(K)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, L)[:K]
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    return c
```
For small n, direct might be more accurate; but used for n>600. If used in `_lbfgs_obj` for n finite. Good.

`_fft_max`:
```
def _fft_max(a):
    n=len(a)
    if n <= 600:
       c=np.convolve(a,a)
       return float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    c=_fft_conv(a)
    return float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
```

`_fft_cross_plus`:
```
def _fft_cross_plus(p, w):
    n=len(p)
    if n==0: return np.array([])
    L=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
    Q=np.fft.rfft(w,L)
    h=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(P)*Q, L)[:n]
    h=np.maximum(h,0.0)
    return h
```
If w length >L? w length 2n-1 =K<L, okay. If w length 0? no.

`_score_fft`:
```
def _score_fft(a):
    arr=_clean_array(a)
    if arr is None: return float('inf'), None
    s=arr.sum()
    if s < 1e-15: return float('inf'), None
    p=arr/s
    M=_fft_max(p)
    score=2.0*len(p)*M
    return float(score), p
```

`_eval_exact`:
```
def _eval_exact(arr):
    try:
        s = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in arr])
    except Exception:
        return float('inf')
    if not np.isfinite(s): return float('inf')
    return float(s)
```

`_try_sequence`:
```
def _try_sequence(a):
    global _BEST_SCORE, _BEST_SEQ
    arr=_clean_array(a)
    if arr is None: return float('inf')
    s=arr.sum()
    if s < 1e-15: return float('inf')
    arr=arr/s
    sc=_eval_exact(arr)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < _BEST_SCORE:
        _BEST_SCORE=float(sc)
        _BEST_SEQ=arr.copy()
        print(f"new best exact score = {_BEST_SCORE:.8f} len = {len(arr)}")
    return sc
```

`_softmax`:
```
def _softmax(x):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64)
    x=np.where(np.isfinite(x),x,0.0)
    m=x.max()
    e=np.exp(x-m)
    s=e.sum()
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(len(x),dtype=np.float64)/len(x)
    return e/s
```

`_project_simplex`:
```
def _project_simplex(v):
    v=np.asarray(v,dtype=np.float64)
    n=len(v)
    if n==0: return v
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css=np.cumsum(u)-1.0
    ind=np.nonzero(u*np.arange(1,n+1)+1.0-css > 0.0)[0]
    if ind.size==0:
       w=np.ones(n)/n; return w
    sigma=ind[-1]
    theta=(css[sigma]-1.0)/(sigma+1.0)
    w=np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
    s=w.sum()
    if s <=0: w=np.ones(n)/n
    else: w/=s
    return w
```
Need check formula: Standard uses `cssv = np.cumsum(v) - 1`; `ind = np.nonzero(v_sorted * np.arange(1,n+1) > cssv)[0][-1]`. Our expression: `u*k +1 - css >0` since css=cumsum-1, condition u*k > css -? Let's derive: condition sorted u_j (j 0-index) with j+1=m: u_{j+1}*(j+1) +1 - (sum_{1..m} u) >0. Standard: (v_{[k]} - θ)*k >0? The code often:
```
cssv = np.cumsum(v) - 1
ind = np.nonzero(v * np.arange(1, v.size+1) > cssv)[0][-1]
theta = (cssv[ind] + 1) / (ind + 1)
return np.maximum(v - theta, 0)
```
Let's use that standard to avoid error. With v sorted descending `u`, `cssv = np.cumsum(u) - 1.0`. Condition `u * np.arange(1,n+1) > cssv`. ind last true. theta=(cssv[ind]+1.0)/(ind+1.0). w=v-theta. Yes. We'll use that.
```
ind = np.nonzero(u * np.arange(1,n+1) > cssv)[0]
if ind.size==0: uniform
theta=(cssv[ind[-1]]+1.0)/(ind[-1]+1.0)
```
Good.

`_resample`:
```
def _resample(a,m,mode=0):
    arr=_clean_array(a)
    if arr is None or m <=1: return None
    n=len(arr)
    s=arr.sum()
    if s < 1e-15: return None
    p=arr/s
    if n == m: return p
    old_x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    new_x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m)
    if mode == 0:
       y=np.interp(new_x, old_x, p)
    else:
       lp=np.interp(new_x, old_x, np.log(p + 1e-300))
       y=np.exp(lp - float(np.max(lp)))
    y=np.maximum(y,0.0)
    sy=y.sum()
    if sy <=0: return None
    return y/sy
```

`_is_similar`:
```
def _is_similar(a,b):
    if a is None or b is None or len(a)!=len(b): return False
    na=float(np.linalg.norm(a)); nb=float(np.linalg.norm(b))
    if na < 1e-12 or nb < 1e-12: return True
    return float(np.linalg.norm(a-b) / na) < 0.02
```

```
def _random_x(n, rng):
    t=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n,endpoint=False)
    x = rng.uniform(-1.5,1.5)*(2.0*t-1.0)
    x += rng.uniform(-0.5,0.5)*(4.0*t*(t-1.0))
    n_terms=int(rng.integers(12,41))
    max_f=max(2,min(200,n//12))
    for _ in range(n_terms):
       f=int(rng.integers(1,max_f+1))
       if f % 2 == 0 and rng.random() < 0.85:
           f += 1
       amp=float(rng.uniform(0.1,2.5)) * (2.0 if f%2==1 else 0.3)
       ph=float(rng.uniform(0.0,2.0*np.pi))
       x += amp*np.cos(np.pi*f*t + ph)
    mx=float(np.max(np.abs(x)))
    if mx > 1.0: x *= 3.0/(mx) # maybe scale to keep max ~3
    return x
```
If mx small, no scale; x range small. Maybe always `x *= 3.0 / max(mx,1.0)`, so max at most3. Good.

`_random_search`:
```
def _random_search(n, max_seconds, pool, rng):
    if max_seconds < 10: return
    deadline=time.monotonic()+max_seconds
    count=0
    max_iter=int(max(100, min(5000, int(max_seconds*40))))
    for alpha in [0.3,0.7,1.5,3.0]:
        p=rng.dirichlet(np.ones(n)*alpha)
        _add_p_scored(pool,p)
        count+=1
    while time.monotonic() < deadline and count < max_iter:
        x=_random_x(n,rng)
        p=_softmax(x)
        _add_p_scored(pool,p)
        count +=1
        if count % 1000 ==0:
            print(f"random n={n} count={count} best pool...")
    print(f"random n={n} iterations={count}")
```
Need if pool cap sort every add. Good.

`_anchor_obj`:
```
def _anchor_obj(x):
    p=_ANCHOR_P
    if p is None:
       return float('inf')
    n=len(p); L=_ANCHOR_L
    q=_softmax(x)
    Fq=np.fft.rfft(q,L)
    K=2*n-1
    c_self=np.fft.irfft(Fq*Fq,L)[:K]
    c_cross=np.fft.irfft(_ANCHOR_PF*Fq,L)[:K]
    vals=np.empty(2*K, dtype=np.float64)
    vals[:K]=np.maximum(c_self,0.0)
    vals[K:]=np.maximum(c_cross,0.0)
    return _lse_val(vals, _LBFGS_TAU)
```

For self, use `_fft_cross_plus(q,w_self)`, which internally rffts q length L. But we already have Fq; could compute h_self = ifft(conj(Fq)*rfft(w_self)L)[:n]. But okay. To reduce, implement inline:
```
Qwself=np.fft.rfft(w_self,L)

```

```
def _anchor_grad(x):
   p; n; L; q; Fq; K
   vals concat; w_all=_lse_weight(vals,_LBFGS_TAU)
   ws=w_all[:K]; wc=w_all[K:]
   Qs=np.fft.rfft(ws,L); hs=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(Fq)*Qs,L)[:n]; hs=np.maximum(hs,0)
   Qc=np.fft.rfft(wc,L); hc=np.fft.irfft(_ANCHOR_PC*Qc,L)[:n]; hc=np.maximum(hc,0)
   tau=_LBFGS_TAU
   g_q=(2.0/tau)*hs + (1.0/tau)*hc
   return q*(g_q - float(np.dot(q,g_q)))
```

`_project_simplex`:
```
def _project_simplex(v):
    v=np.asarray(v,dtype=np.float64)
    n=len(v)
    if n==0: return v
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    S=np.cumsum(u)
    idx=np.nonzero(u * np.arange(1,n+1) > S - 1.0)[0]
    if idx.size == 0:
       w=np.ones(n)/n; return w
    k=idx[-1]
    theta=(S[k]-1.0)/(k+1.0)
    w=np.maximum(v-theta,0.0)
    s=w.sum()
    if s <=0: w=np.ones(n)/n
    else: w/=s
    return w
```
Test v=[2,0,0,0] sum2: u [2,0,0,0], S[2,2,2,2]. cond k1:2>1 true; k2:0>1 false; θ=(2-1)/1=1, w[1,0,0,0], sum1. Good.
If v sum already1 and all nonneg, condition? v=[.2,.8,0]; S[.8,1.0,1.0]; cond k1:.8> -0.2 true; k2:1.6>0 true; k3:0>0 false? 0 >0 false; k=1 θ=(1-1)/2=0, w=v. Good. If sum1 but with zeros and condition last? okay.

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function coefficients minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / sum(a)^2.

Strategy:
- Use FFT-based self-convolution and log-sum-exp smoothing for a corrected
  global L-BFGS objective/gradient on the probability simplex.
- Start from any provided good sequence, resampled variants, analytic symbols,
  and smooth random Fourier-type initializations.
- Use full-dimensional L-BFGS refinement, an anchor-based LSE refinement
  (minimizing max(a*q, q*q)), and an exact multi-start projected/multiplicative
  subgradient polish for final improvements.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_BEST_SCORE = float('inf')
_BEST_SEQ = None

_LBFGS_TAU = 1e-6

_ANCHOR_P = None
_ANCHOR_L = 0
_ANCHOR_PF = None
_ANCHOR_PC = None


def _score_key(item):
    return item[0]


def _next_pow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _clean_array(a):
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.minimum(arr, 1000.0)
    return arr


def _fft_conv(a):
    n = int(len(a))
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    K = 2 * n - 1
    L = _next_pow2(K)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, L)[:K]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c


def _fft_max(a):
    n = int(len(a))
    if n <= 600:
        c = np.convolve(a, a)
        return float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    c = _fft_conv(a)
    return float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0


def _fft_cross_plus(p, w):
    """
    h_i = sum_j p_j w_{i+j}, with zero padding.
    Used for gradients of weighted sums of convolution coefficients.
    """
    n = int(len(p))
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    L = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    Q = np.fft.rfft(w, L)
    h = np.fft.irfft(np.conj(P) * Q, L)[:n]
    h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
    return h


def _score_fft(a):
    arr = _clean_array(a)
    if arr is None:
        return float('inf'), None
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        return float('inf'), None
    p = arr / s
    M = _fft_max(p)
    return float(2.0 * len(p) * M), p


def _eval_exact(arr):
    try:
        s = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in arr])
    except Exception:
        return float('inf')
    if not np.isfinite(s):
        return float('inf')
    return float(s)


def _try_sequence(a):
    global _BEST_SCORE, _BEST_SEQ
    arr = _clean_array(a)
    if arr is None:
        return float('inf')
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        return float('inf')
    arr = arr / s
    sc = _eval_exact(arr)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < _BEST_SCORE:
        _BEST_SCORE = float(sc)
        _BEST_SEQ = arr.copy()
        print(f"new best exact score = {_BEST_SCORE:.8f}  len = {len(arr)}")
    return sc


def _softmax(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    m = float(np.max(x)) if x.size else 0.0
    e = np.exp(x - m)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(len(x), dtype=np.float64) / max(1, len(x))
    return e / s


def _lse_val(c, tau):
    cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if tau <= 0.0:
        return cmax
    z = (c - cmax) / tau
    zmax = float(np.max(z)) if z.size else 0.0
    vals = np.exp(z - zmax)
    return float(cmax + tau * (zmax + np.log(float(vals.sum()))))


def _lse_weight(c, tau):
    cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if tau <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros(len(c), dtype=np.float64)
        if w.size:
            w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return w
    z = (c - cmax) / tau
    zmax = float(np.max(z)) if z.size else 0.0
    w = np.exp(z - zmax)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        w = np.zeros(len(c), dtype=np.float64)
        if w.size:
            w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        w /= s
    return w


def _lbfgs_obj(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    p = _softmax(x)
    c = _fft_conv(p)
    return _lse_val(c, _LBFGS_TAU)


def _lbfgs_grad(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    tau = max(1e-15, float(_LBFGS_TAU))
    p = _softmax(x)
    c = _fft_conv(p)
    w = _lse_weight(c, tau)
    h = _fft_cross_plus(p, w)
    g_p = (2.0 / tau) * h
    return p * (g_p - float(np.dot(p, g_p)))


def _refine_lbfgs(p, max_seconds):
    global _LBFGS_TAU
    arr = _clean_array(p)
    if arr is None:
        return None
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        return None
    p = arr / s
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
    p /= float(p.sum())
    if max_seconds <= 5.0:
        return p

    n = len(p)
    x = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    x = np.clip(x, -80.0, 80.0)
    x -= float(np.mean(x))

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_m = _fft_max(p)
    best_x = x.copy()
    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(max_seconds)

    factors = [0.5, 0.25, 0.12, 0.06, 0.03, 0.015, 0.008, 0.004]
    for factor in factors:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        ref_M = max(float(best_m), 1e-12)
        tau = max(1e-12, float(factor) * ref_M)
        _LBFGS_TAU = float(tau)

        if n > 7000:
            opts = {'maxiter': 120, 'maxfun': 250, 'ftol': 1e-12, 'gtol': 1e-9, 'maxls': 20}
        else:
            opts = {'maxiter': 220, 'maxfun': 450, 'ftol': 1e-12, 'gtol': 1e-9, 'maxls': 25}

        try:
            res = minimize(_lbfgs_obj, x, jac=_lbfgs_grad,
                           method='L-BFGS-B', options=opts)
            x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        except Exception:
            break

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
            break

        x = np.clip(x, -80.0, 80.0)
        x -= float(np.mean(x))
        p2 = _softmax(x)
        p2 = np.maximum(p2, 0.0)
        s2 = float(p2.sum())
        if s2 <= 0.0:
            break
        p2 /= s2

        m2 = _fft_max(p2)
        if np.isfinite(m2) and m2 < best_m:
            best_m = m2
            best_p = p2.copy()
            best_x = x.copy()
        else:
            if np.isfinite(m2) and m2 > best_m * 1.05:
                x = best_x.copy()
            # keep current x otherwise; it may still be useful

    return best_p


def _project_simplex(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(v)
    if n == 0:
        return v
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    S = np.cumsum(u)
    idx = np.nonzero(u * np.arange(1, n + 1) > S - 1.0)[0]
    if idx.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    k = int(idx[-1])
    theta = (S[k] - 1.0) / float(k + 1)
    w = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        w = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    else:
        w /= s
    return w


def _resample(a, m, mode=0):
    arr = _clean_array(a)
    if arr is None or m <= 1:
        return None
    n = len(arr)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        return None
    p = arr / s
    if n == m:
        return p

    old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    if mode == 0:
        y = np.interp(new_x, old_x, p)
    else:
        logp = np.interp(new_x, old_x, np.log(p + 1e-300))
        y = np.exp(logp - float(np.max(logp)))

    y = np.maximum(y, 0.0)
    sy = float(y.sum())
    if sy <= 0.0:
        return None
    return y / sy


def _prep_opt_start(a):
    arr = _clean_array(a)
    if arr is None:
        return None
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        return None
    p = arr / s
    n = len(p)
    if n < 1536:
        r = _resample(p, 2048, 0)
        if r is not None:
            return r
        return p
    if n > 9000:
        r = _resample(p, 8192, 0)
        if r is not None:
            return r
        return p
    return p


def _is_similar(a, b):
    if a is None or b is None or len(a) != len(b):
        return False
    na = float(np.linalg.norm(a))
    nb = float(np.linalg.norm(b))
    if na < 1e-12 or nb < 1e-12:
        return True
    return float(np.linalg.norm(a - b) / na) < 0.02


def _add_to_pool(pool, arr, score, cap=40):
    if arr is None or not np.isfinite(score):
        return
    pool.append((float(score), np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).copy()))
    if len(pool) > cap:
        pool.sort(key=_score_key)
        del pool[cap:]


def _add_p_scored(pool, p_arr, cap=40):
    sc, pnorm = _score_fft(p_arr)
    _add_to_pool(pool, pnorm, sc, cap)


def _random_x(n, rng):
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    x = float(rng.uniform(-1.5, 1.5)) * (2.0 * t - 1.0)
    x += float(rng.uniform(-0.5, 0.5)) * (4.0 * t * (t - 1.0))

    n_terms = int(rng.integers(12, 41))
    max_f = max(2, min(200, n // 12))
    for _ in range(n_terms):
        f = int(rng.integers(1, max_f + 1))
        if f % 2 == 0 and rng.random() < 0.85:
            f += 1
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.1, 2.5)) * (2.0 if f % 2 == 1 else 0.3)
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        x += amp * np.cos(np.pi * f * t + ph)

    mx = float(np.max(np.abs(x)))
    if mx > 0.0:
        x *= 3.0 / max(1.0, mx)
    return x


def _random_search(n, max_seconds, pool, rng):
    if max_seconds <= 5.0:
        return
    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(max_seconds)
    count = 0
    max_iter = int(max(100, min(5000, int(float(max_seconds) * 40))))

    for alpha in [0.3, 0.7, 1.5, 3.0]:
        p = rng.dirichlet(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) * alpha)
        _add_p_scored(pool, p)
        count += 1

    while time.monotonic() < deadline and count < max_iter:
        x = _random_x(n, rng)
        p = _softmax(x)
        _add_p_scored(pool, p)
        count += 1
        if count % 1000 == 0:
            print(f"random search n={n} count={count}")

    print(f"random search n={n} done count={count}")


def _add_common_candidates(n, pool, cap=40):
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)

    _add_p_scored(pool, np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), cap)

    for a in [-2.0, -1.0, -0.5, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0]:
        _add_p_scored(pool, np.exp(a * (2.0 * t - 1.0)), cap)
        _add_p_scored(pool, np.exp(a * np.cos(np.pi * t)), cap)
        _add_p_scored(pool, np.exp(a * np.cos(np.pi * (t - 0.25))), cap)
        _add_p_scored(pool, np.exp(a * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t)), cap)

    for aa in [0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0]:
        for bb in [0.2, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0]:
            p = np.power(t + 1e-8, aa) * np.power(1.0 - t + 1e-8, bb)
            _add_p_scored(pool, p, cap)

    for a in [-1.5, -0.75, -0.25, 0.25, 0.75, 1.5]:
        p = 1.0 + a * (2.0 * t - 1.0)
        p = np.maximum(p, 1e-6)
        _add_p_scored(pool, p, cap)
        q = 1.0 + a * (2.0 * t - 1.0) + 0.25 * a * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * t)
        q = np.maximum(q, 1e-6)
        _add_p_scored(pool, q, cap)

    for a in [1.0, 2.0, 4.0]:
        p = 1.0 + a * (t - 0.5) * np.cos(np.pi * t)
        p = np.maximum(p, 1e-6)
        _add_p_scored(pool, p, cap)


def _set_anchor(p):
    global _ANCHOR_P, _ANCHOR_L, _ANCHOR_PF, _ANCHOR_PC
    arr = _clean_array(p)
    if arr is None:
        return
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        return
    arr = arr / s
    n = len(arr)
    L = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    Pf = np.fft.rfft(arr, L)
    _ANCHOR_P = arr.copy()
    _ANCHOR_L = int(L)
    _ANCHOR_PF = Pf.copy()
    _ANCHOR_PC = np.conj(Pf).copy()


def _anchor_obj(x):
    p = _ANCHOR_P
    if p is None:
        return float('inf')
    n = len(p)
    L = _ANCHOR_L
    q = _softmax(x)
    Fq = np.fft.rfft(q, L)
    K = 2 * n - 1

    c_self = np.fft.irfft(Fq * Fq, L)[:K]
    c_cross = np.fft.irfft(_ANCHOR_PF * Fq, L)[:K]

    vals = np.empty(2 * K, dtype=np.float64)
    vals[:K] = np.maximum(c_self, 0.0)
    vals[K:] = np.maximum(c_cross, 0.0)
    return _lse_val(vals, _LBFGS_TAU)


def _anchor_grad(x):
    p = _ANCHOR_P
    if p is None:
        return np.zeros_like(x)
    n = len(p)
    L = _ANCHOR_L
    tau = max(1e-15, float(_LBFGS_TAU))

    q = _softmax(x)
    Fq = np.fft.rfft(q, L)
    K = 2 * n - 1

    c_self = np.fft.irfft(Fq * Fq, L)[:K]
    c_cross = np.fft.irfft(_ANCHOR_PF * Fq, L)[:K]

    vals = np.empty(2 * K, dtype=np.float64)
    vals[:K] = np.maximum(c_self, 0.0)
    vals[K:] = np.maximum(c_cross, 0.0)
    w_all = _lse_weight(vals, tau)
    w_self = w_all[:K]
    w_cross = w_all[K:]

    Qs = np.fft.rfft(w_self, L)
    h_self = np.fft.irfft(np.conj(Fq) * Qs, L)[:n]
    h_self = np.maximum(h_self, 0.0)

    Qc = np.fft.rfft(w_cross, L)
    h_cross = np.fft.irfft(_ANCHOR_PC * Qc, L)[:n]
    h_cross = np.maximum(h_cross, 0.0)

    g_q = (2.0 / tau) * h_self + (1.0 / tau) * h_cross
    return q * (g_q - float(np.dot(q, g_q)))


def _refine_anchor(anchor_p, max_seconds):
    global _LBFGS_TAU
    arr = _clean_array(anchor_p)
    if arr is None:
        return None
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        return None
    arr = arr / s
    _set_anchor(arr)
    if _ANCHOR_P is None:
        return None
    if max_seconds <= 5.0:
        return arr

    p = arr.copy()
    x = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    x = np.clip(x, -80.0, 80.0)
    x -= float(np.mean(x))

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_m = _fft_max(p)
    best_x = x.copy()
    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(max_seconds)

    n = len(p)
    factors = [0.35, 0.18, 0.09, 0.045, 0.022, 0.011]
    for factor in factors:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        ref_M = max(float(best_m), 1e-12)
        tau = max(1e-12, float(factor) * ref_M)
        _LBFGS_TAU = float(tau)

        if n > 7000:
            opts = {'maxiter': 120, 'maxfun': 250, 'ftol': 1e-12, 'gtol': 1e-9, 'maxls': 20}
        else:
            opts = {'maxiter': 200, 'maxfun': 420, 'ftol': 1e-12, 'gtol': 1e-9, 'maxls': 25}

        try:
            res = minimize(_anchor_obj, x, jac=_anchor_grad,
                           method='L-BFGS-B', options=opts)
            x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        except Exception:
            break

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
            break

        x = np.clip(x, -80.0, 80.0)
        x -= float(np.mean(x))
        p2 = _softmax(x)
        p2 = np.maximum(p2, 0.0)
        s2 = float(p2.sum())
        if s2 <= 0.0:
            break
        p2 /= s2

        m2 = _fft_max(p2)
        if np.isfinite(m2) and m2 < best_m:
            best_m = m2
            best_p = p2.copy()
            best_x = x.copy()
        else:
            if np.isfinite(m2) and m2 > best_m * 1.05:
                x = best_x.copy()

    return best_p


def _polish_subgrad(p, max_seconds, rng):
    arr = _clean_array(p)
    if arr is None:
        return None
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        return None
    p = arr / s
    if max_seconds <= 3.0:
        return p

    n = len(p)
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
    p /= float(p.sum())

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_m = _fft_max(p)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(max_seconds)

    top_k_base = max(24, min(300, n // 20))
    step_factor = 0.2
    no_improve = 0
    iters = 0
    iters_limit = 20000

    while time.monotonic() < deadline and iters < iters_limit:
        iters += 1

        p = np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
        p /= float(p.sum())

        c = _fft_conv(p)
        M = float(np.max(c))
        if M < best_m:
            best_m = M
            best_p = p.copy()
            no_improve = 0

        use_all = (no_improve > 5 and iters % 5 == 0) or (iters % 17 == 0)
        if use_all:
            denom = max(float(M) * 10.0, 1e-30)
            lam = np.exp((c - M) / denom)
            slam = float(lam.sum())
            if slam <= 0.0:
                lam = np.zeros(len(c))
                lam[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
            else:
                lam /= slam
        else:
            frac = float(rng.uniform(0.02, 0.30))
            kk = int(max(8, min(400, top_k_base * float(rng.uniform(0.5, 2.0)))))
            thresh = max(0.0, M * (1.0 - frac))
            idx = np.flatnonzero(c >= thresh)
            if idx.size < 4:
                idx = np.argsort(c)[-kk:]
            elif idx.size > kk:
                idx = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:kk]
            vals = c[idx] - thresh
            sv = float(vals.sum())
            if sv <= 0.0:
                vals = np.ones(len(idx), dtype=np.float64)
            lam = np.zeros(len(c), dtype=np.float64)
            lam[idx] = vals / float(vals.sum())

        h = _fft_cross_plus(p, lam)
        g = h - float(np.mean(h))
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(g)))
        if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale < 1e-15:
            break

        base = max(1e-6, step_factor * M / scale)
        trials = [base * 0.25, base * 0.5, base, base * 2.0, base * 4.0]

        best_trial_score = best_m
        best_trial_p = None

        for st in trials:
            if st <= 0.0:
                continue

            # multiplicative / mirror step
            rel = -st * g
            rel -= float(np.max(rel))
            e = np.exp(np.clip(rel, -50.0, 50.0))
            p2 = p * e
            s2 = float(p2.sum())
            if s2 > 0.0:
                p2 = p2 / s2
                m2 = _fft_max(p2)
                if m2 < best_trial_score:
                    best_trial_score = m2
                    best_trial_p = p2

            # Euclidean projected step
            p3 = _project_simplex(p - st * g)
            s3 = float(p3.sum())
            if s3 > 0.0:
                p3 = p3 / s3
                m3 = _fft_max(p3)
                if m3 < best_trial_score:
                    best_trial_score = m3
                    best_trial_p = p3

        if best_trial_p is not None and best_trial_score < best_m - 1e-16:
            p = best_trial_p
            p = np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
            p /= float(p.sum())
            best_m = best_trial_score
            best_p = p.copy()
            no_improve = 0
            if step_factor < 1.0:
                step_factor = min(1.0, step_factor * 1.4)
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if step_factor > 0.01:
                step_factor = max(0.01, step_factor * 0.75)

            if no_improve > 35:
                logp = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
                improved = False
                for noise in [0.004, 0.01, 0.02, 0.04]:
                    p2 = _softmax(logp + rng.normal(0.0, noise, n))
                    p2 = np.maximum(p2, 1e-300)
                    p2 /= float(p2.sum())
                    m2 = _fft_max(p2)
                    if m2 < best_m:
                        best_m = m2
                        best_p = p2.copy()
                        p = p2.copy()
                        improved = True
                        break
                if not improved:
                    # very small move toward the current best (no change normally)
                    pass
                no_improve = 0

    return best_p


def propose_candidate(budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    global _BEST_SCORE, _BEST_SEQ

    start = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(30.0, float(budget_s))
    safety = 45.0 if budget > 120.0 else 3.0
    deadline = start + max(10.0, budget - safety)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(12345)

    # Baseline valid sequence.
    _try_sequence(np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64))

    # Use previously known construction if present.
    parent = None
    try:
        hs = globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
        if hs is not None:
            parent = _clean_array(hs)
            if parent is not None:
                _try_sequence(parent)
                for m in [2048, 4096, 8192]:
                    if time.monotonic() > deadline - 80:
                        break
                    for mode in (0, 1):
                        r = _resample(parent, m, mode)
                        if r is not None:
                            _try_sequence(r)
    except Exception as e:
        print("parent init failed:", e)

    pool = []
    if _BEST_SEQ is not None:
        _add_p_scored(pool, _BEST_SEQ, 40)

    # Deterministic analytic candidates.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 700:
        _add_common_candidates(4096, pool, 40)

    # Random smooth candidates.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 520:
        tb1 = min(120.0, max(20.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.08))
        _random_search(4096, tb1, pool, rng)

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 380:
        tb2 = min(60.0, max(10.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.04))
        _random_search(2048, tb2, pool, rng)

    # Exact-evaluate a handful of internal best candidates.
    if pool:
        pool.sort(key=_score_key)
        n_exact_top = min(20, len(pool))
        for i in range(n_exact_top):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 520:
                break
            _try_sequence(pool[i][1])

    # Full L-BFGS polish from several good starts.
    now = time.monotonic()
    reserve = max(220.0, (deadline - now) * 0.18)
    refine_budget = max(0.0, deadline - now - reserve - 40.0)
    max_starts = int(min(5, max(1, refine_budget / 180.0 + 1)))

    starts = []
    if _BEST_SEQ is not None:
        st = _prep_opt_start(_BEST_SEQ)
        if st is not None:
            starts.append(st)

    if pool:
        for sc, arr in pool:
            if len(starts) >= max_starts:
                break
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - reserve - 80:
                break
            st = _prep_opt_start(arr)
            if st is None:
                continue
            same = False
            for o in starts:
                if _is_similar(st, o):
                    same = True
                    break
            if same:
                continue
            starts.append(st)

    if not starts:
        starts.append(_softmax(_random_x(4096, rng)))

    if len(starts) > 0 and refine_budget > 40.0:
        per = max(30.0, refine_budget / float(len(starts)))
        for idx, p0 in enumerate(starts):
            remaining_here = deadline - time.monotonic() - reserve - 40.0
            if remaining_here <= 30.0:
                break
            t_use = max(20.0, min(per, remaining_here))
            print(f"L-BFGS restart {idx + 1}/{len(starts)} len={len(p0)} time={t_use:.0f}s")
            q = _refine_lbfgs(p0, t_use)
            if q is not None:
                _try_sequence(q)

    # Try resampling the current exact best to other useful lengths.
    if _BEST_SEQ is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 160:
        cur_len = len(_BEST_SEQ)
        for m in [4096, 6144, 8192]:
            if m == cur_len:
                continue
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 160:
                break
            for mode in (0, 1):
                r = _resample(_BEST_SEQ, m, mode)
                if r is not None:
                    _try_sequence(r)

    # Anchor-constrained refine on the best exact sequence.
    if _BEST_SEQ is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 200:
        arr = _prep_opt_start(_BEST_SEQ)
        if arr is not None:
            q = _refine_anchor(arr, min(150.0, max(30.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 90.0)))
            if q is not None:
                _try_sequence(q)

    # Another full L-BFGS polish on the current best if time remains.
    if _BEST_SEQ is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 150:
        arr = _prep_opt_start(_BEST_SEQ)
        if arr is not None:
            q = _refine_lbfgs(arr, min(180.0, max(30.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 80.0)))
            if q is not None:
                _try_sequence(q)

    # Final exact subgradient polishing.
    if _BEST_SEQ is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 70:
        arr = _prep_opt_start(_BEST_SEQ)
        if arr is not None:
            q = _polish_subgrad(arr, min(260.0, max(30.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 40.0)), rng)
            if q is not None:
                _try_sequence(q)

        if pool and time.monotonic() < deadline - 70:
            arr2 = _prep_opt_start(pool[0][1])
            if arr2 is not None and not _is_similar(arr2, _BEST_SEQ):
                q = _polish_subgrad(arr2, min(120.0, max(20.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 30.0)), rng)
                if q is not None:
                    _try_sequence(q)

    if _BEST_SEQ is None:
        if pool:
            _BEST_SEQ = _prep_opt_start(pool[0][1])
        if _BEST_SEQ is None:
            _BEST_SEQ = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)

    final = _clean_array(_BEST_SEQ)
    if final is None or final.size == 0:
        final = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(final.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        final = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)
        s = 1024.0
    final = final / s

    print(f"final tracked exact score = {_BEST_SCORE if np.isfinite(_BEST_SCORE) else float('inf'): .8f}  len = {len(final)}")
    return [float(v) for v in final]
```
